Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the energy framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 152. The resulting operator achieves bound O(865/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the energy space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 152 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 865. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the energy structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 919. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 37 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on energy structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 919. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 37. QED.
